Nothing here logs a password.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport secrets\nimport sqlite3\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom argon2 import PasswordHasher\nfrom argon2.exceptions import InvalidHashError, VerifyMismatchError\n\nROLES = (\"admin\", \"editor\")\n\n_SCHEMA = \"\"\"\ncreate table if not exists users (\n    id integer primary key,\n    email text not null unique,\n    display_name text not null,\n    title text not null default '',\n    term text not null default '',\n    role text not null check (role in ('admin', 'editor')),\n    is_active integer not null default 1,\n    password_hash text not null,\n    must_change_password integer not null default 0\n)\n\n\ndef init_db(database: Path) -> None:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        conn.execute(_SCHEMA)\n        columns = {r[\"name\"] for r in conn.execute(\"pragma table_info(users)\")}\n        if \"must_change_password\" not in columns:\n            conn.execute(\"alter table users add column must_change_password\"\n                         \" integer not null default 0\")\n        conn.execute(_SETTINGS_SCHEMA)\n\n\ndef create_user(database: Path, *, email: str, password: str, role: str,\n                display_name: str, title: str = \"\", term: str = \"\") -> int:\n    if role not in ROLES:\n        raise ValueError(f\"role must be one of {ROLES}\")\n    problem = password_problem(password)\n\"\"\"T01: login, logout and CSRF, tested through the HTTP app.\"\"\"\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom conftest import DEMO_USERS, token_from\n\n\ndef log_in(client, email, password, **extra):\n    token = token_from(client.get(\"/login\").text)\n    return client.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": email, \"password\": password,\n                                       \"csrf_token\": token, **extra},\n                       follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef test_seeded_users_log_in_and_reach_the_console(client_as):\n    for role in (\"admin\", \"editor\"):\n        response = client_as(role).get(\"/admin\")\n        assert response.status_code == 200\n        assert \"hello admin\" in response.text.lower()\n\n\ndef test_wrong_password_and_unknown_email_get_the_same_refusal(client):\n    wrong = log_in(client, DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"][\"email\"], \"not-the-password\")\n    unknown = log_in(client, \"nobody@example.test\", \"not-the-password\")\n    assert wrong.status_code == unknown.status_code == 200\n    assert \"invalid email or password\" in wrong.text.lower()\n    assert \"invalid email or password\" in unknown.text.lower()\n\n\ndef test_failed_login_does_not_start_a_session(client):\n    log_in(client, DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"][\"email\"], \"not-the-password\")\n    assert client.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False).status_code in (302, 303)\n\n\ndef test_anonymous_admin_request_redirects_to_login(client):\n    response = client.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False)\n    assert response.status_code in (302, 303)\n    assert response.headers[\"location\"].endswith(\"/login\")\n    assert \"hello admin\" not in docs/transcripts/iphs400_mp2-cms_chat-session_16_faisal-syed_20261006.md\n?? docs/transcripts/iphs400_mp2-cms_chat-session_17_faisal-syed_20261006.md    assert c.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False).status_code in (302, 303)\n\n\ndef test_voluntary_password_change_works_for_any_user(client_as, make_client):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    form = c.get(\"/user/password\")\n    assert form.status_code == 200\n    new = \"brand-new-password-9\"\n    r = c.post(\"/user/password\", data={\n        \"current_password\": EDITOR[\"password\"], \"new_password\": new,\n        \"confirm_password\": new, \"csrf_token\": token_from(form.text)},\n        follow_redirects=False)\n    assert r.status_code == 303\n    assert log_in(make_client, EDITOR[\"email\"], new)[1].status_code == 303\n\n\ndef test_minimum_enforced_when_creating_a_user(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    r = admin.post(\"/admin/users\", data={\n        \"email\": \"new@example.test\", \"display_name\": \"New\", \"role\": \"editor\",\n                  DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"][\"password\"])[1].status_code == 303\n\n\ndef test_anonymous_cannot_reset(client):\n    r = client.post(\"/admin/users/1/reset-password\", data={},\n                    follow_redirects=False)\n    assert r.status_code in (302, 303, 403)\n\n\ndef test_reset_unknown_user_is_404(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    assert admin.post(\"/admin/users/999/reset-password\",\n                      data={\"csrf_token\": csrf(admin)}).status_code == 404\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef cli_db(db_path, monkeypatch):\n    monkeypatch.setattr(cli.settings, \"DATABASE_PATH\", db_path)\n    return db_path\n\n\ndef answers(monkeypatch, *values):\n    it = iter(values)\n    monkeypatch.setattr(cli.getpass, \"getpass\", lambda prompt=\"\": next(it))\n\n\ndef test_cli_resets_admin_own_password(cli_db, make_client, monkeypatch):\n    answers(monkeypatch, \"a-fresh-admin-pw1\", \"a-fresh-admin-pw1\")\n    assert cli.main([\"reset-password\", \"Admin@Example.test\"]) == 0\n    c, r = log_in(make_client, \"admin@example.test\", \"a-fresh-admin-pw1\")\n    assert r.status_code == 303\n    assert c.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False).status_code == 200\n    assert log_in(make_client, User changing their own password (also the forced change after a reset).\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import users\nfrom app.security import csrf_token, logged_in_user, verify_csrf\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/user\")\n\n\ndef render(request: Request, user, error: str | None = None, status_code: int = 200):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/change_password.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Change password\", \"user\": user, \"error\": error,\n         \"csrf_token\": csrf_token(request), \"min_password\": users.MIN_PASSWORD,\n         \"forced\": bool(user[\"must_change_password\"])}, status_code=status_code)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/password\")\ndef password_form(request: Request, user=Depends(logged_in_user)):\n    return render(request, user)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/password\", dependencies=[Depends(verify_csrf)])\ndef change_password(request: Request, user=Depends(logged_in_user),\n                    current_password: str = Form(\"\"), new_password: str = Form(\"\"),\n                    confirm_password: str = Form(\"\")):\n    def fail(message: str):\n        return render(request, user, message, 400)\n\n    if not users.verify_password(user, current_password):\n        return fail(\"Your current password is not correct.\")\n    problem = users.password_problem(new_password)\n    if problem:\n        return fail(problem)\n    if new_password != confirm_password:\n        return fail(\"The new passwords do not match.\")\n    if new_password == current_password:\n        return fail(\"Choose a password different from the current one.\")\n    users.set_password(request.app.state.database, user[\"id\"], new_password)\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin\", status_code=303)\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Temporary password</h1>\n  <p>Temporary password for {{ target.display_name }} ({{ target.email }}):</p>\n  <p><code data-temp-password=\"{{ temp_password }}\">{{ temp_password }}</code></p>\n  <p><strong>This is shown only once.</strong> Give it to them now; they must choose their own password at next login. {"parentUuid":"b64439b1-0a23-4a53-ac45-db477547ea51","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"cb4c69d5-c06a-428b-aac0-2d339c3608ee","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01ExWBXhvzRTbiaXnZjFX18e","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"T02: Posts, tested through the HTTP app.\"\"\"\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app import users\nfrom app.main import create_app\nfrom conftest import token_from\n\n\ndef save_post(c, title=\"Rush night\", body=\"Hello **world**\", slug=\"\", action=\"save\",\n              post_id=None, page=\"/admin/posts/new\", **extra):\n    \"\"\"Submit the post form; return the response (redirects not followed).\"\"\"\n    path = f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\" if post_id else \"/admin/posts\"\n    token = token_from(c.get(page).text)\n    return c.post(path, data={\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body\": body,\n                              \"action\": action, \"csrf_token\": token, **extra},\n                  follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef created_id(response):\n    assert response.status_code == 303, response.text\n    return int(re.search(r\"/admin/posts/(\\d+)\", response.headers[\"location\"]).group(1))\n\n\ndef post_form(c, post_id, path_suffix, **data):\n    token = token_from(c.get(\"/admin/posts/new\").text)\n    return c.post(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}{path_suffix}\",\n                  data={\"csrf_token\": token, **data}, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef other_editor(db_path, make_client):\n    users.create_user(db_path, email=\"other@example.test\", password=\"other-editor-pw\",\n                      role=\"editor\", display_name=\"Other Editor\")\n    c = make_client()\n    token = token_from(c.get(\"/login\").text)\n    c.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": \"other@example.test\",\n    page = c.get(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\").text\n    assert \"sneaky\" not in page  # editor's slug change ignored once locked\n    assert 'name=\"slug\"' in page and \"disabled\" in page.split('name=\"slug\"')[1][:80] \\\n        or \"readonly\" in page.split('name=\"slug\"')[1][:80]\n\n\ndef test_admin_can_unlock_a_slug_after_first_publish(client_as):\n    editor, admin = client_as(\"editor\"), client_as(\"admin\")\n    post_id = created_id(save_post(editor, title=\"Locked\", action=\"publish\"))\n    assert post_form(editor, post_id, \"/unlock-slug\").status_code == 403\n    assert post_form(admin, post_id, \"/unlock-slug\").status_code == 303\n    save_post(editor, title=\"Locked\", slug=\"fixed-slug\", post_id=post_id)\n    assert \"fixed-slug\" in editor.get(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\").text\n\n\ndef test_editing_a_published_post_keeps_it_published(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    post_id = created_id(save_post(c, title=\"Live-ish\", action=\"publish\"))\n    save_post(c, title=\"Live-ish fixed\", post_id=post_id)\n    page = c.get(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\").text\n    assert \"Live-ish fixed\" in _search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":656,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":4,"output_tokens":1115,"cache_read_input_tokens":92655,"cache_creation_input_tokens":656,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":656},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard","fallback_credit":null},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"thinkingDurationMs":1917,"serverClassifierRequest":"124c20a9-7200-4984-98d9-f7dc8398fabc","apiBlockIndex":0,"requestId":"req_011CfmsiPEipNqDkdkXiPtSt","type":"assistant","uuid":"480b772f-e52b-4a00-8fa0-5b6baa6d12fa","timestamp":"2026-10-06T23:46:41.431Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"4b1a37e3-ea11-48e9-baa9-b66b752b7a0d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"5f10d906-37da-4c41-a22b-b19971eb0f9f","version":"2.1.292","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"480b772f-e52b-4a00-8fa0-5b6baa6d12fa","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfmsiPZa9t7rePKi9rBx9","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01GGkRNFq4qUjFZrxoGUmfEt","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"gh issue comment 7 --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n**Flaky `test_temp_password_login_must_change_before_anything_else` — root cause and fix**\n\n**Cause: environment clock + app session handling; not the test.** The test logic is deterministic. The failure was a silently lost session: `client_as` accepts a 200 from a failed login, then the next request is bounced to `/login`, so the reset page never shows the temp password. The same flake showed up in other tests (`test_editor_cannot_reset_anyones_password`, `test_posts::test_admin_can_unlock_a_slug_after_first_publish`).\n\nStarlette's session cookie is signed with itsdangerous `TimestampSigner`, which rejects any cookie stamped even 1s *ahead* of the current clock (\"Signature age < 0\"). On this WSL2 machine the wall clock steps backwards (measured: `int(time.time())` went backwards twice and drifted up to ~5.9s from the monotonic clock in 60s). A step between login and the next request invalidates the session.\n\nRuled out: the reset route, temp-password generation, CSRF/cookie round-trip (4000 iterations clean), and plain login (600 iterations clean). Deterministic repro: login, then make `time.time` read 2s earlier -> `/admin` returns 303; 2s later -> 200.\n\n**Fix:** `SkewTolerantSigner` / `SkewTolerantSessionMiddleware` in `app/security.py` accept a validly signed cookie up to 5 minutes ahead of the local clock (signature is still verified; far-future cookies are still refused). Regression test: `tests/test_auth.py::test_session_survives_the_clock_stepping_backwards` (fails without the fix, passes with it).\n\n**Verification:** full suite 3 consecutive runs, 166 passed each.\nEOF\n)\" && git add app/security.py app/main.py tests/test_auth.py && git commit -q -m \"T06: Tolerate backward clock steps in session cookies (fixes flaky login-dependent tests)\n\nSession cookies are signed with itsdangerous TimestampSigner, which rejects a\ncookie stamped ahead of the local clock. On WSL2 the wall clock steps\nbackwards, silently logging users (and tests) out. Accept validly signed\ncookies up to 5 minutes ahead; add a regression test.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Sonnet 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>\" && git push 2>&1 | tail -3; git status --short | grep -v transcripts"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":656,"cache_read_input_tokens":92655,"output_tokens":1115,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":190},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":656,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":4,"output_tokens":1115,"cache_read_input_tokens":92655,"cache_creation_input_tokens":656,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":656},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard","fallback_credit":null},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_01GGkRNFq4qUjFZrxoGUmfEt":{"command":"cd /home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms && gh issue comment 7 --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n**Flaky `test_temp_password_login_must_change_before_anything_else` — root cause and fix**\n\n**Cause: environment clock + app session handling; not the test.** The test logic is deterministic. The failure was a silently lost session: `client_as` accepts a 200 from a failed login, then the next request is bounced to `/login`, so the reset page never shows the temp password. The same flake showed up in other tests (`test_editor_cannot_reset_anyones_password`, `test_posts::test_admin_can_unlock_a_slug_after_first_publish`).\n\nStarlette's session cookie is signed with itsdangerous `TimestampSigner`, which rejects any cookie stamped even 1s *ahead* of the current clock (\"Signature age < 0\"). On this WSL2 machine the wall clock steps backwards (measured: `int(time.time())` went backwards twice and drifted up to ~5.9s from the monotonic clock in 60s). A step between login and the next request invalidates the session.\n\nRuled out: the reset route, temp-password generation, CSRF/cookie round-trip (4000 iterations clean), and plain login (600 iterations clean). Deterministic repro: login, then make `time.time` read 2s earlier -> `/admin` returns 303; 2s later -> 200.\n\n**Fix:** `SkewTolerantSigner` / `SkewTolerantSessionMiddleware` in `app/security.py` accept a validly signed cookie up to 5 minutes ahead of the local clock (signature is still verified; far-future cookies are still refused). Regression test: `tests/test_auth.py::test_session_survives_the_clock_stepping_backwards` (fails without the fix, passes with it).\n\n**Verification:** full suite 3 consecutive runs, 166 passed each.\nEOF\n)\" && git add app/security.py app/main.py tests/test_auth.py && git commit -q -m \"T06: Tolerate backward clock steps in session cookies (fixes flaky login-dependent tests)\n\nSession cookies are signed with itsdangerous TimestampSigner, which rejects a\ncookie stamped ahead of the local clock. On WSL2 the wall clock steps\nbackwards, silently logging users (and tests) out. Accept validly signed\ncookies up to 5 minutes ahead; add a regression test.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Sonnet 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>\" && git push 2>&1 | tail -3; git status --short | grep -v transcripts"}},"wireIngestContext":{"toolu_01GGkRNFq4qUjFZrxoGUmfEt":{"cwd":"/home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms"}},"serverClassifierRequest":"124c20a9-7200-4984-98d9-f7dc8398fabc","apiBlockIndex":1,"requestId":"req_011CfmsiPEipNqDkdkXiPtSt","type":"assistant","uuid":"b30a1b13-0ea3-4684-b35a-1f0057b032b6","timestamp":"2026-10-06T23:46:45.136Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"4b1a37e3-ea11-48e9-baa9-b66b752b7a0d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"5f10d906-37da-4c41-a22b-b19971eb0f9f","version":"2.1.292","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"4babd447-c934-429a-ba4d-9e303c443cd7","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"deferred_tools_record","entries":[],"toolInputCopies":[{"id":"toolu_01GGkRNFq4qUjFZrxoGUmfEt","copy":"wire"}]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"68e1d193-b0cb-47ae-afaa-95fbc642e99a","timestamp":"2026-10-06T23:46:55.859Z","session_id":"4b1a37e3-ea11-48e9-baa9-b66b752b7a0d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"5f10d906-37da-4c41-a22b-b19971eb0f9f","version":"2.1.292","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"68e1d193-b0cb-47ae-afaa-95fbc642e99a","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfmsjJpr5qMtgHrFkscMM","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The flake is fixed, committed as 6d82da5 and pushed. The full suite passed 3 runs in a row, 166 tests each.\n\n**Where the bug is:** not in the test. It's the environment and the app together. The test logic is deterministic. The flake happens when a user's login session is silently lost partway through a test.\n\n**Root cause:** the session cookie is signed with a timestamp. Starlette's `itsdangerous` signer rejects any cookie stamped even 1 second ahead of the current clock. On your WSL2 machine the wall clock steps backwards. I measured it going backwards and drifting up to about 5.9 seconds in 60 seconds. If that happens between login and the next request, the session is dropped.\n\nIn the failing run the login worked but the session was dropped on the next request, so the reset page never showed the temp password. `client_as` treats a 200 from a failed login as success, which hid the cause.\n\nThis affected other tests too. I saw `test_editor_cannot_reset_anyones_password` fail, and `test_posts::test_admin_can_unlock_a_slug_after_first_publish` failed in a clean sequential full-suite run.\n\nI confirmed it by making the clock read 2 seconds earlier after login: `/admin` returned 303 instead of 200. I also ruled out the reset route, temp-password generation, the CSRF/cookie round-trip (4000 iterations) and plain login (600 iterations).\n\n**Fix:** `app/security.py` now has a signer, wired in through `app/main.py`, that accepts a validly signed cookie up to 5 minutes ahead of the local clock. The signature is still checked, and cookies stamped further ahead are still refused. The regression test is `test_session_survives_the_clock_stepping_backwards` in `tests/test_auth.py`. It fails without the fix and passes with it.\n\nThree clean runs is modest evidence on its own, since the flake was intermittent. The deterministic regression test is the stronger proof.\n\nI commented on #7, which is the T06 ticket. The commit starts with `T06:` and has no `Closes #`, because the ticket was already closed. I left `notes/usage-ledger.csv` and the transcript files out of the commit."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1890,"cache_read_input_tokens":93311,"output_tokens":723,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":0},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1890,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":723,"cache_read_input_tokens":93311,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1890,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1890},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard","fallback_credit":null},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"serverClassifierRequest":"49dfc6fd-6b46-4f1c-a76e-75575e49d26f","apiBlockIndex":0,"requestId":"req_011CfmsjJYEWjx7TFwD3W5D1","type":"assistant","uuid":"c5240c77-99fe-4db6-9719-8c193674b830","timestamp":"2026-10-06T23:46:55.858Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"4b1a37e3-ea11-48e9-baa9-b66b752b7a0d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"5f10d906-37da-4c41-a22b-b19971eb0f9f","version":"2.1.292","gitBranch":"main"}
